# Skills · C · part 29

920 entries, causal-linker to caveman-compress. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **causal-linker**: Maps actions, events, and side-effects into causal chains. Builds "X causes Y" relations across the codebase. Triggers — "extract causal linker", "brownfield causal linker", "/causal-linker". ([ForgePlan/marketplace](https://github.com/ForgePlan/marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/forgeplan-brownfield-pack/skills/causal-linker) · ★4)
- **causal-loop-diagram**: Map reinforcing and balancing feedback loops, mark delays and archetypes, and surface leverage points that linear cause-and-effect misses. Use when a fix keeps making its problem worse, you need to see where to intervene, or the system's behavior comes from its structure, not a single cause. ([aleknitka/agent-creative-thinking-skills](https://github.com/aleknitka/agent-creative-thinking-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/creative-thinking-skills/skills/causal-loop-diagram))
- **causal-loop-diagram-generator**: Creates interactive causal-loop diagram (CLD) articles for MkDocs Material sites. Use when the user wants to visualize feedback loops, systems archetypes (reinforcing/balancing loops, limits to growth, tragedy of the commons), or any topic with competing runaway and stabilizing dynamics. Produces vis-network diagrams — not static images or Mermaid. ([dmccreary/ibook-skills](https://github.com/dmccreary/ibook-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/archived/causal-loop-diagram-generator) · ★113 · 2 copies)
- **causal-loop-diagrams**: Map cause-and-effect relationships with reinforcing/balancing feedback loops to visualize system structure and predict behavior patterns ([lev-os/agents](https://github.com/lev-os/agents/tree/HEAD/skills-db/thinking/patterns/causal-loop-diagrams) · ★27)
- **causal-loop-trace**: Use this skill when the user invokes /causal-loop-trace or asks for a default public reasoning trace using Causal Loop Reasoning. Treat the scale as a rough effort target rather than a fixed quota: around 100 numbered lines or roughly 2,000 to 3,500 tokens of public scratchpad detail. Use this skill when the user wants the answer shaped by systems dynamics instead of a generic response. ([cerredz/Vidbyte-Skills](https://github.com/cerredz/Vidbyte-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/causal-loop-trace))

- **causal-map**: Extract and explain the causal map of a ProbeMatrix investigation - how organisational influences enabled supervisory failures, preconditions and unsafe acts - using the factor links the AI wrote into each finding's causal explanation, even when the export's Causal Chain sheet is empty. Produces a Mermaid diagram, a ranked list of causal pathways and a plain-language narrative. Use when someone asks how the factors connect, for a causal chain, causal tree, root-cause pathway, "how did the organisation set this up", or wants a diagram of the investigation. ([ashley-eyekyam/probematrix-learnings-coach](https://github.com/ashley-eyekyam/probematrix-learnings-coach/tree/HEAD/skills/causal-map))

Use when user mentions matching, propensity score, observational study, confounders, selection bias, or covariate balance. Not for settings with unobserved confounding. ([RobsonTigre/everyday-causal-skills](https://github.com/RobsonTigre/everyday-causal-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/causal-matching) · ★84)

- **causal-model-construction**: Before intervening in a system, write the explicit causal chain from the proposed action to the intended outcome — every arrow marked verified or assumed — and check the weakest assumed arrow first. ([ralfyishere/piensalo](https://github.com/ralfyishere/piensalo/tree/HEAD/micro-skills/causal-model-construction) · ★1)

- **causal-query-classifier**: Pearl's three-rung causal hierarchy as a query classifier. Tags every analytics question as rung-1 (association, P(Y|X)), rung-2 (intervention, P(Y|do(X))), or rung-3 (counterfactual, P(Y_x|Y',X')) before answering. Refuses to escalate a rung-1 observational finding into a rung-2 ship/kill recommendation without naming an identification strategy (back-door, instrumental variable, DiD, RDD, synthetic control). Use this skill whenever interpreting an analytics question that asks why or what-if, to classify it on Pearl's causal hierarchy before answering. Pairs with analytics-diagnostic-method.… ([clamp-sh/analytics-skills](https://github.com/clamp-sh/analytics-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/causal-query-classifier) · ★10)

Use this skill when the user wants the answer shaped by cause and effect analysis instead of a generic response. ([cerredz/Vidbyte-Skills](https://github.com/cerredz/Vidbyte-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/causal-trace-small))

- **cavecrew-reviewer**: Diff/branch/file reviewer. Use for "review this PR", "review my diff", "audit this file", or any request to inspect code for bugs, risks, and correctness issues. - **cavekit-audit**: Audita en profundidad un dominio (seguridad, privacidad, rendimiento, accesibilidad o cumplimiento): arma un plan exhaustivo, lo revisa contra la app real y lo divide en tareas de solo análisis que escriben un único informe. El análisis largo corre en un subagente y solo vuelve el informe. Usala ante pedidos de auditoría o revisión amplia. NO usarla para una funcionalidad (cavekit-plan + cavekit-build), un bug puntual (cavekit-backprop) ni una revisión de un solo diff (code-review).
